#     PROJECT                         STP>Solar Terrestrial Probes
#     SOURCE_NAME                     STA>Solar Terrestrial Relations Observatory of the Sun-Earth Line
#     DISCIPLINE                      Solar Physics>Heliospheric Physics
#     DATA_TYPE                       l2-magplasma-1m>Level-2 combined Mag and Plasma 1-minute average
#     DESCRIPTOR                      impact>In-situ Measurements of Particles and CME Transients_Magnetometer
#     DATA_VERSION                    1
#     PI_NAME                         Christina Lee
#     PI_AFFILIATION                  UCB/SSL
#     TEXT                            The file contains Level 2 magnetic field vectors from the IMPACT/MAG, solar                           
#                                      wind parameters and space craft position data from the PLASTIC STEREO Ahead                           
#                                      spacecraft.                                                                                           
#     INSTRUMENT_TYPE                 Magnetic Fields (space)
#                                     Plasma and Solar Wind
#     MISSION_GROUP                   STEREO
#     LOGICAL_SOURCE                  STA_L2_MAGPLASMA_1m
#     LOGICAL_L0SOURCE                STA_L1_MAG
#     LOGICAL_PLASTIC                 STA_L2_PLA_1DMax_1min_V01
#     LOGICAL_FILE_ID                 STA_L2_MAGPLASMA_1m_2024_V07
#     LOGICAL_SOURCE_DESCRIPTION      STEREO Ahead IMPACT/MAG Magnetic Field and PLASTIC Solar Wind Plasma Data
#     TIME_RESOLUTION                 00:01:00.000
#     GENERATED_BY                    Hanying Wei
#     GENERATION_DATE                 2025-12-03
#     MODS                            Started from March 1,2020, velocity vectors are no longer available from PLASTIC                      
#                                      team resulting in flagged data in Vr_Over_V_RTN, Vt_Over_V_RTN, Vn_Over_V_RTN,                        
#                                      Vp_RTN. The calculation formula for Variable Dynamic_Pressure has been changed                        
#                                      from using Variable Vp_RTN (solar wind proton speed) to using Variable Vp (proton                     
#                                      bulk speed) in response to that no sensible value of Vp_RTN has been available                        
#                                      since the above date.                                                                                 
#                                     Started from November 19, 2025, Variable RTN position has been replaced with                          
#                                      Variable HCI position. Updated several global and variable attributes according                       
#                                      to ISTP guidelines.                                                                                   
#     LINK_TEXT                       Experiment and Data descriptions for STEREO/IMPACT
#                                     STEREO Data Server at UCLA
#     LINK_TITLE                      STEREO/IMPACT PI Site
#                                     UCLA Site
#     HTTP_LINK                       http://sprg.ssl.berkeley.edu/impact
#                                     https://stereo.epss.ucla.edu
#     FILE_NAMING_CONVENTION          source_datatype_descriptor
